MODEL     : z-ai/glm-5.3-flash
METRIC    : area_delay_product
RESULT    : PASS  |  best cost: 3.98e+06 area_delay_product  (eval 12)
STEPS: 60  |  duration: 7882.6s

[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Full 8x8 2-D IDCT of a baseline JPEG decoder (ultraembedded/core_jpeg), module
name `jpeg_idct_2d`: the row pass (jpeg_idct_x, OUT_SHIFT=11), a double-buffered
corner-turn (jpeg_idct_transpose with four 32x32 dual-port RAMs), and the column
pass (jpeg_idct_y, OUT_SHIFT=15), composed exactly as in the original decoder
(downstream ready tied high; the row pass accepts no backpressure). Each 1-D
pass is the factorized (AAN-style) algorithm with cosine constants scaled by
4096 (C1..C7 = 4017, 3784, 3406, 2896, 2276, 1567, 799), time-sharing 5
multipliers over an 8-beat schedule. ALL arithmetic is evaluated in 32-bit
signed context with wrap-around; outputs of each pass are arithmetic-shifted
right (11 then 15); the 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling is `(x * 181) / 256` where
`*` wraps at 32 bits and `/` is SIGNED division truncating toward zero. This
exact fixed-point behavior is what the golden vectors encode.

Interface (clocked, sync active-high reset `rst`; no backpressure):
  inputs : clk, rst, img_start_i (1-cycle pulse resets the output streamers),
           img_end_i (unused), inport_valid_i, inport_idx_i[2:0],
           inport_data0..3_i[15:0]
  outputs: outport_valid_o, outport_data_o[31:0], outport_idx_o[5:0]

Input protocol (the upstream block serializer's FIXED schedule, reproduced
exactly by the testbench): per row of 8 coefficients c0..c7 (16-bit signed),
8 back-to-back valid beats with idx = 0..7; the four data lanes carry the EVEN
set (c0,c2,c4,c6) on beats idx in {0,2,5,7} and the ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7) on
beats idx in {1,3,4,6}. 8 rows back-to-back form a 64-beat block; between
blocks there are >= 2 idle cycles (valid=0, idx=0, data=0). Pipeline registers
advance every clock regardless of valid — designs must be robust to the gap
pattern exactly as the original is.

Output (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on outport_valid_o; the
k-th streamed value of a block carries outport_idx_o = {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the
column pass emits column-major; the idx label gives the ROW-MAJOR pixel
position) and must match the expected output at that position. The stream of a
block overlaps the next block's input beats (the transpose double-buffers one
full block).

A working starting point is provided: `context/starting_point.v` contains the
ORIGINAL core_jpeg modules (Apache 2.0), unmodified, under a thin composition
top. It passes all golden vectors (they were generated from it — the original
RTL is the correctness oracle). Study it, then optimize while preserving its
exact behavior. Sibling benchmark: `jpeg_idct_2d_spire` (identical tb.sv +
vectors.dat, bit-exact SpireHDL starting point). The 1-D row pass alone is
also available as the `jpeg_idct_x_*` benchmarks.

NOTE on memories: the cost-evaluation synthesis flow targets a standard-cell
library with no RAM macros, so every behavioral memory in this design (the
transpose buffers, block_out, etc.) is inferred as a register array plus mux
decode REGARDLESS of coding style. Optimizing that decode/mux logic is fair
game; instantiating a hard RAM macro is not possible in this flow.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT of the baseline JPEG decoder
// (ultraembedded/core_jpeg, Apache 2.0): the verbatim composition of
// jpeg_idct_x -> jpeg_idct_transpose (double-buffered corner-turn RAMs)
// -> jpeg_idct_y, exactly as instantiated in the original jpeg_idct.v
// (downstream ready tied high; the x-pass accepts no backpressure).
// The submodules below are the ORIGINAL core_jpeg sources, unmodified.

module jpeg_idct_2d
(
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for jpeg_idct_2d — the full 8x8 2-D IDCT
// (x-pass -> transpose -> y-pass) of ultraembedded/core_jpeg.
// Reads vectors.dat: one line per 8x8 block = 64 input coefficients (16-bit
// signed, 4 hex digits, row-major c[row][col]) followed by 64 expected outputs
// (32-bit, 8 hex digits, in ROW-MAJOR pixel order — outputs are matched via
// outport_idx_o, which labels each streamed value with its row-major position).
//
// Drive protocol: identical to the jpeg_idct_x benchmark (the upstream block
// serializer's fixed schedule): per row 8 back-to-back valid beats idx=0..7,
// EVEN coefficient set (c0,c2,c4,c6) at idx in {0,2,5,7}, ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7)
// at idx in {1,3,4,6}; 8 rows back-to-back per 64-beat block; >= 2 idle cycles
// between blocks (valid=0, idx=0, data=0).
//
// Output contract (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on
// outport_valid_o; the k-th value of a block carries
// outport_idx_o == {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the y-pass un-transpose permutation) and
// must equal the expected output at that row-major position. Comparison is
// EXACT (integer outputs).
module tb;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic        clk;
  logic        rst;
  logic        img_start;
  logic        inport_valid;
  logic [15:0] inport_data0, inport_data1, inport_data2, inport_data3;
  logic [2:0]  inport_idx;
  logic        outport_valid;
  logic [31:0] outport_data;
  logic [5:0]  outport_idx;

  jpeg_idct_2d dut (
    .clk(clk), .rst(rst),
    .img_start_i(img_start), .img_end_i(1'b0),
    .inport_valid_i(inport_valid),
    .inport_data0_i(inport_data0), .inport_data1_i(inport_data1),
    .inport_data2_i(inport_data2), .inport_data3_i(inport_data3),
    .inport_idx_i(inport_idx),
    .outport_valid_o(outport_valid),
    .outport_data_o(outport_data),
    .outport_idx_o(outport_idx)
  );

  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  // ---- load vectors.dat: flat token stream, 128 tokens per block ----------
  localparam MAX_BLOCKS = 1024;
  logic [15:0] coeffs [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  logic [31:0] expy   [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  int num_blocks;

  task automatic load_vectors;
    int fd, rc, n;
    logic [31:0] tok;
    logic [31:0] flat [0:MAX_BLOCKS*128-1];
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    n = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      rc = $fscanf(fd, "%h", tok);
      if (rc == 1) begin
        flat[n] = tok;
        n = n + 1;
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);
    num_blocks = n / 128;
    if (num_blocks * 128 != n)
      $display("WARNING: %0d trailing tokens ignored", n - num_blocks * 128);
    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++)
      for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) begin
        coeffs[b*64+i] = flat[b*128+i][15:0];
        expy[b*64+i]   = flat[b*128+64+i];
      end
  endtask

  // ---- output checker -------------------------------------------------------
  int out_cnt;
  bit checking;
  logic [5:0] k;

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    #1;
    if (checking && outport_valid) begin
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      k = out_cnt % 64;
      if (outport_idx !== {k[2:0], k[5:3]}) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d out=%0d idx=%0d expected_idx=%0d",
                 out_cnt / 64, out_cnt % 64, outport_idx, {k[2:0], k[5:3]});
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
      if (outport_data !== expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx]) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d pos=%0d expected_y=%08x actual_y=%08x",
                 out_cnt / 64, outport_idx, expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx],
                 outport_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
`ifdef DUMP_Y
      $display("Y %02x %08x", outport_idx, outport_data);
`endif
      out_cnt = out_cnt + 1;
    end
  end

  // ---- driver ----------------------------------------------------------------
  task automatic drive_beat(input logic valid, input logic [2:0] kk,
                            input logic [15:0] d0, d1, d2, d3);
    @(negedge clk);
    inport_valid = valid;
    inport_idx   = kk;
    inport_data0 = d0;
    inport_data1 = d1;
    inport_data2 = d2;
    inport_data3 = d3;
  endtask

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    out_cnt = 0;
    checking = 0;
    rst = 1;
    img_start = 0;
    inport_valid = 0;
    inport_idx = 0;
    inport_data0 = 0; inport_data1 = 0; inport_data2 = 0; inport_data3 = 0;

    load_vectors();

    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 1;             // one-cycle image-start pulse
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 0;
    checking = 1;

    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++) begin
      for (int r = 0; r < 8; r++) begin
        for (int kb = 0; kb < 8; kb++) begin
          if (kb == 0 || kb == 2 || kb == 5 || kb == 7)
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],

... (showing lines 1-147 of 169; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
  -> read_file(filename='starting_point.v', limit=700, offset=148)

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 3/60]
    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C3_16 = 3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C4_16 = 2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C6_16 = 1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C7_16 = 799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);



reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

wire [31:0] outport_data0_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data1_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data2_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data3_w;

wire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);
wire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);
wire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);
wire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram0
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr0_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data0_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram1
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr1_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data1_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram2
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr2_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data2_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram3
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr3_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data3_w)
);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else if (img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// FSM
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
localparam STATE_W           = 2;
localparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;

reg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;
reg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;

always @ *
begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    case (state_q)
    STATE_IDLE:
    begin
        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)
            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    end
    STATE_SETUP:
    begin
        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    end
    STATE_ACTIVE:
    begin
        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)
            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
    end
    default: ;
    endcase

    if (img_start_i)
        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
end

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;
else
    state_q <= next_state_r;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])
    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: ;
    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

assign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);
assign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];
assign outport_data0_o = outport_data0_w;
assign outport_data1_o = outport_data1_w;
assign outport_data2_o = outport_data2_w;
assign outport_data3_o = outport_data3_w;


endmodule

reg [31:0] ram_read0_q;
reg [31:0] ram_read1_q;


// Synchronous write
always @ (posedge clk0_i)
begin
    if (wr0_i)
        ram[addr0_i][31:0] <= data0_i[31:0];

    ram_read0_q <= ram[addr0_i];

module jpeg_idct_y
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Params
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
#(
     parameter OUT_SHIFT        = 15
    ,parameter INPUT_WIDTH      = 32
)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Ports
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C3_16 = 3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C4_16 = 2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C6_16 = 1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C7_16 = 799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

[ASSISTANT]
Now I understand the design well. My analysis of the original reveals several big optimization opportunities:

1. **The 5 multipliers per pass are `32×32` variable multipliers** (because `mul_b` is a register), but the B side only ever holds the 7 constants C1..C7 → replace with 7 **fixed-constant multipliers** (one per constant, operand muxed by schedule).
2. **The `x/256` signed division** can become `(x<0) ? (x+255)>>>8 : x>>>8`.
3. **Width narrowing with rigorous bounds**: 1-D pass outputs fit in 20 bits signed (max |out| = 709e6>>11 = 346k), so the transpose RAMs can be 20-bit wide instead of 32 (saves 1536 FFs), and final outputs fit 17 bits.
4. Beat 3/4 loads are duplicates → skip; `ptr_conv` 64-case function is just a bit permutation `{ptr[2:0], ptr[5:3]}`; the RAM's port-0 read is dead.

Let me write the optimized design:

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 10/60]
Created file: design_v1.v

[ASSISTANT]
The baseline passes at cost 9.5M (delay 2222 ps dominated by the 32-bit RAM read muxes; area dominated by ten 32×32 variable multipliers). Now I'll write my optimized version. Key ideas:

1. **Replace the 10 variable 32×32 multipliers with 14 constant multipliers** (operand register per constant — the constant per schedule beat is fixed).
2. **Drop the redundant beat-4 loads** (the duplicated operand loads) by recomputing products continuously.
3. **Provable width narrowing**: `>>>11` of any 32-bit value fits in 21 bits; `>>>15` fits in 17 bits → x-pass outputs/transpose RAMs become 21-bit, final outputs 17-bit.
4. **Signed `/256` → add/shift** (truncation toward zero).
5. **`ptr_conv` 64-case function → bit permutation** `{ptr[2:0], ptr[5:3]}` (verified equal).
6. Simplified single-write/read-registered RAM (dead port-0 read removed).
  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (ultraembedded/core_jpeg algorithm).\n// Optimized: narrowed datapath [ASSISTANT]
The critical path is the transpose RAM read mux (high fanout on `rd_addr_q` + deep 32:1 mux). Meanwhile CLK-to-Q seems to dominate (~1400 ps), suggesting delay has a floor — so both **delay (fanout/mux depth)** and **area (multipliers)** matter. Let me (a) probe the synthesis tradeoff with a relaxed target, and (b) build a v2 that replaces the RAM read mux with a registered **one-hot AND-OR read** (fanout 4 per one-hot bit, shallow OR tree).

[ASSISTANT]
Excellent data points. The eval confirms: **delay is pinned at 2853 ps by the transpose RAM read path** (a ~2 ns net delay on the high-fanout `rd_addr_q` output + deep 32:1 mux), and relaxing `target_delay` doesn't help (Yosys just sizes up drivers). Area barely moved (4000 vs 4273).

Key insight from analyzing the read schedule: the read address is always `{half, parity(b), j}` — a pure function of the beat counter. So I'll restructure the RAM read as a **registered one-hot decode + AND-OR** (every fanout ≤ 21, and the decode/mux split across 2 pipeline stages), delaying the transpose's `valid/idx` by one cycle to keep triples aligned. Also narrowing the x-pass datapath (products 29b, butterflies 30b — provably wrap-free except the intentional ×181 wrap).
  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (ultraembedded/core_jpeg algorithm).\n// v2: transpose RAM read = registered one-hot decode + shallow AND-OR\n// (fanout-bounded, no deep 32:1 mux), narrowed x-pass datapath\n// (provably wrap-free except the intentional x181 wrap), constant\n// multipliers, signed /256 as add+shift, idx as bit concat.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_2d\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input p3, p4, p5, p6, p7;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    p1 <= q1;\n    p2 <= q2;\n    p3 <= q3;\n    p4 <= q4;\n    p5 <= q5;\n    p6 <= q6;\n    p7 <= [ASSISTANT]
**Major win: cost 4.46M (2.13× better than the 9.5M baseline)!** The one-hot RAM read fixed the critical path. Now the critical path is the transpose decode → RAM → y-pass input adder → `i0`. Since the one-hot enable decode is *shared* across all 4 RAMs (same read address), I can register it once (32 FFs), pull the decode out of the data path, and delay the transpose's valid/idx by one cycle to stay aligned (the RAM read data is now effectively one cycle later):

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// 32-entry RAM: sync write + one-hot enabled registered read
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
module jpeg_idct_transpose_ram #(
     parameter DATA_W = 21
)
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr0_i
    ,input  [DATA_W-1:0]  data0_i
    ,input           wr0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  en1_i

    // Outputs
    ,output [DATA_W-1:0]  data1_o
);

reg [DATA_W-1:0] ram [0:31];
reg [DATA_W-1:0] rd_q;

wire [DATA_W-1:0] anded [0:31];
wire [DATA_W-1:0] or2   [0:15];
wire [DATA_W-1:0] or4   [0:7];
wire [DATA_W-1:0] or8   [0:3];
wire [DATA_W-1:0] or16  [0:1];

genvar gq;
generate
    for (gq = 0; gq < 32; gq = gq + 1)
        assign anded[gq] = {DATA_W{en1_i[gq]}} & ram[gq];
    for (gq = 0; gq < 16; gq = gq + 1)
        assign or2[gq] = anded[2*gq] | anded[2*gq+1];
    for (gq = 0; gq < 8; gq = gq + 1)
        assign or4[gq] = or2[2*gq] | or2[2*gq+1];
    for (gq = 0; gq < 4; gq = gq + 1)
        assign or8[gq] = or4[2*gq] | or4[2*gq+1];
    for (gq = 0; gq < 2; gq = gq + 1)
        assign or16[gq] = or8[2*gq] | or8[2*gq+1];
endgenerate

wire [DATA_W-1:0] rd_c = or16[0] | or16[1];

always @(posedge clk)
begin
    if (wr0_i)
        ram[addr0_i] <= data0_i;

    rd_q <= rd_c;
end

assign data1_o = rd_q;

endmodule

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Early (one-cycle-ahead) read address + registered one-hot decode.
// E(c) = A(c+1) where A is the read address actually used at cycle c;
// the decode of A(c) is therefore available as a register one cycle
// early, so the RAM read is a pure AND-OR of the stored words.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [ 3:0] rd_addr_early_q;   // E
reg [31:0] rd_en_q;           // decode({block_rd_q, A})

always @(posedge clk)
if (rst)
    rd_addr_early_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_early_q <= 4'd8;            // A(next active cycle)
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_early_q <= 4'd8;            // A(first active cycle)
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])                // E(k) = A(k+1) = f(k-1)
    3'd0: rd_addr_early_q <= rd_addr_early_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_early_q <= rd_addr_early_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: rd_addr_early_q <= rd_addr_early_q;
    3'd3: rd_addr_early_q <= rd_addr_early_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_early_q <= rd_addr_early_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_early_q <= rd_addr_early_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_early_q <= rd_addr_early_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_early_q <= rd_addr_early_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

wire [15:0] oh16_w = 16'b0000000000000001 << rd_addr_early_q;
wire [31:0] oh32_w = block_rd_q ? {oh16_w, 16'b0} : {16'b0, oh16_w};

always @(posedge clk)
    rd_en_q <= oh32_w;

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @(posedge clk)
if (rst || img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

// Early (one-cycle-ahead) read-enable decode. The enable sampled by
// the posedge ending cycle c must decode the address in use during
// cycle c (rd_addr_q + delta(rd_idx_q)); registering that decode

        3'd1: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
        3'd2: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q;

[ASSISTANT]
Now I see the precise off-by-one: `rd_en_q` during cycle *c* is loaded from the decode evaluated during cycle *c−1*, but the RAM's `rd_q` captures `andor(ram, rd_en_q)` at the posedge ending *c*. So `rd_en_q` during cycle *c−1* must decode `A(c)` — which is computable using the **+1-shifted case index** (`case(rd_idx_q+1)`) applied to the current `rd_addr_q`. One-character-class fix:
  -> edit_file(filename='design_v4.v', new_string="    if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)\n    begin\n        // rd_en_q(c+1) must decode A(c+1) = f(rd_addr_q(c), case(c+1)):\n        // the case select is shifted by +1 so the registered decode is\n        // ready one cycle before the corresponding RAM read\n        case (rd_idx_q[2:0] + 3'd1)\n        3'd0: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd1: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd2: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q;\n        3'd3: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd4: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd5: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd6: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q + 4'd1;\n        3'd7: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        endcase\n    end", old_string="    if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)\n    begin\n        case (rd_idx_q[2:0])\n        3'd0: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd1: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd2: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q;\n        3'd3: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd4: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd5: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd6: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q + 4'd1;\n        3'd7: nxt_addr_w = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        endcase\n    end")

[ASSISTANT]
Now I understand the pipeline timing deeply. The 1262 ps path is the y-pass operand-capture mux: the case-select gates the `i0` adder inputs (ABC merged them), so the path = idx reg → decode → **full 21-bit adder** → reg. My fix: capture the even set into a small register bank, compute the two `i0` sums **unconditionally** (no select in their path), and load the ×C4 operand from those registered sums two beats later (the schedule permits — verified against the original's alignment). Also switching pipeline tags to registered one-hot to kill the 3→8 decodes:
  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (ultraembedded/core_jpeg algorithm).\n// v5: operand capture restructured — the even coefficient set is\n// banked into registers, the (c0+c4)/(c0-c4) sums are computed\n// unconditionally (no select gating the adder), and the xC4 operand\n// is loaded from those registered sums (schedule shifted by the\n// pipeline lag, semantically identical to the original). Products are kept\n// at 32 bits so wrapping matches the original 32-bit context.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [31:0] p1, p2, p3, p4, p5, p6, p7;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    p1 <= a1 * C1;\n    p2 <= a2 * C2;\n    p3 <= a3 * C3;\n    p4 <= a4 * C4;\n    p5 <= a5 * C5;\n    p6 <= a6 * C6;\n    p7 <= a7 * C7;\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipeline tags (one-hot)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg        stg0_valid_q, stg1_valid_q, stg2_valid_q, stg3_valid_q;\nreg [ 7:0] stg0_oh_q, stg1_oh_q, stg2_oh_q, stg3_oh_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst) begin\n        stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;\n        stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;\n        stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;\n        stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;\n        stg1_valid_q <= stg0_valid_q;\n        stg2_valid_q <= stg1_valid_q;\n        stg3_valid_q <= stg2_valid_q;\n    end\n    stg0_oh_q <= oh;\n    stg1_oh_q <= stg0_oh_q;\n    stg2_oh_q <= stg1_oh_q;\n    stg3_oh_q <= stg2_oh_q;\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Butterfly / accumulator stage\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_s6;\nreg signed [31:0] o_s7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t0;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t1;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t2;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t3;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t4;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;\n\n// signed division by 256 truncating toward zero\nfunction signed [31:0] d256;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    begin\n        d256 = x[31] ? STATE_SETUP)\n    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)\nbegin\n    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])\n    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n    3'd2: rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n    endcase\nend\n\n// output pipeline stage: breaks the RAM read -> IDCT input combinational\n// path (the whole stream shifts by one cycle)\nreg              valid_d_q;\nreg [ 2:0]       idx_d_q;\nreg [ 7:0]       oh_d_q;\nreg [DATA_W-1:0] data0_d_q, data1_d_q, data2_d_q, data3_d_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_d_q <= 1'b0;\nelse\nbegin\n    valid_d_q <= outport_data2_w;\n    data3_d_q <= outport_data3_w;\nend\n\nassign outport_valid_o  = valid_d_q;\nassign outport_idx_o    = idx_d_q;\nassign outport_oh_o     = oh_d_q;\nassign outport_data0_o  = read\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose_ram #(\n     parameter DATA_W = 21\n)\n(\n    // Inputs\n     input           clk\n    ,input  [ [DATA_W-1:0] rd_q;\n\n// two-level one-hot decode of the read address (low fanout)\nwire [3:0] hh = 4'b0001 << {addr1_i[4], addr1_i[3]};\nwire [7:0] kk = 8'b00000001 << addr1_i[2:0];\n\nwire [31:0] en;\ngenvar gi, gj;\ngenerate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 4; gi = gi + 1)\n        for (gj = 0; gj < 8; gj = gj + 1)\n            assign en[gi*8+gj] = hh[gi] & kk[gj];\nendgenerate\n\nwire [DATA_W-1:0] 1)\n        assign or4[gq] = anded[gq*4+0] | anded[gq*4+1] | anded[gq*4+2] | anded[gq*4+3];\nendgenerate\n\nwire [DATA_W-1:0] rd_c;\ngenvar gg;\ngenerate\n    for (gg = 0; gg < DATA_W; gg = gg + 1)\n **New best: 3.98M** (from 9.5M baseline). The critical path is now the `×181 → +255 → >>>8` chain at beat 7. Since `o_s5/o_s6` are written at beat 7 but not read until beat 5 of the next row (and the output stage reads them 2 cycles after the write), I can pipeline the multiply into its own stage — write `w_q <= t6_5×181` at beat 7, then `o_s5 <= d256(w_q)` at beat 0:

Products are kept\n// at 32 bits so wrapping matches the original 32-bit context.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [31:0] p1, p2, p3, p4, p5, p6, p7;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    p1 <= a1 * C1;\n    p2 <= a2 * C2;\n    p3 <= a3 * C3;\n    p4 <= a4 * C4;\n    p5 <= a5 * C5;\n    p6 <= a6 * C6;\n    p7 <= a7 * C7;\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipeline tags (one-hot)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg        stg0_valid_q, stg1_valid_q, stg2_valid_q, stg3_valid_q;\nreg        stg4_valid_q;\nreg [ 7:0] stg0_oh_q, stg1_oh_q, stg2_oh_q, stg3_oh_q;\nreg [ 7:0] stg4_oh_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst) begin\n        stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;\n        stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;\n        stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;\n        stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;\n        stg4_valid_q <= 1'b0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;\n        stg1_valid_q <= stg0_valid_q;\n        stg2_valid_q <= stg1_valid_q;\n        stg3_valid_q <= stg2_valid_q;\n        stg4_valid_q <= stg3_valid_q;\n    end\n    stg0_oh_q <= oh;\n    stg1_oh_q <= stg0_oh_q;\n    stg2_oh_q <= stg1_oh_q;\n    stg3_oh_q <= stg2_oh_q;\n    stg4_oh_q <= stg3_oh_q;\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Butterfly / accumulator stage\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_s6;\nreg signed [31:0] o_s7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t0;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t1;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t2;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t3;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t4;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;\nreg signed [31:0] s4_q;\n\n// signed division by 256 truncating toward zero\nfunction signed [31:0] d256;\n    input signed [31:0] x;\n    begin\n        d256 = x[31] ? ((x + 32'sd255) >>> 8) : (x >>> 8);\n    end\nendfunction\n\n// x*181 in the original 32-bit wrapping context, staged:\n//   beat 7: w_q <= x*181 (32-bit wrap)\n//   beat 0: o_s5/s6 <= d256(w_q) [ASSISTANT]
The failure is now clear: delaying the output stage breaks the original's same-cycle register aliasing — at output beat 3, `o_t0` must still hold `t0` (the beat-2 value), but one cycle later it already holds `s4` (the beat-4 write). Fix: give the output stage its own registered copy of `t0` (written at butterfly beat 2), keeping everything else:

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 52/60]
Edited file: design_v7.v

[ASSISTANT]
The v7 failure is a subtle scheduling bug: I tied the divide (`d256`) to beat 0 of the *next* row, but for the **last row of each block** the next beat 0 comes after the inter-block gap — *after* the output stage needs it. Fix: fire the divide on its own tag (one cycle after beat 7, gap-independent). The timeline then works out exactly: multiply at beat 7 → divide at beat 7+1 → output-stage beat 7 reads it at beat 7+2:
  -> edit_file(filename='design_v7.v', new_string="reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;\nreg signed [31:0] t0o_q;   // registered copy of t0 for the (delayed) output stage\n\n// divide fires one cycle after the x181 multiply, on its own tag\n// (independent of inter-block gaps)\nreg       div_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    div_q <= 1'b0;\nelse\n    div_q <= stg2_oh_q[7];", old_string='reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;\nreg signed [31:0] t0o_q;   // registered copy of t0 for the (delayed) output stage')

[Step 54/60]
Edited file: design_v7.v

[ASSISTANT]
I now fully understand the original's per-beat register semantics (I traced every write/read cycle-by-cycle). The clean solution: keep the output stage at `stg3` (no extra delay), and split the `×181 → /256` chain across the **beat-6 → beat-7 boundary** (multiply at beat 6 using `t6±t5` directly, divide at beat 7). The output stage reads `o_s5` one cycle after the beat-7 write, so timing is preserved exactly:

rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n    endcase\nend\n\n// output pipeline stage: breaks the RAM read -> IDCT input combinational\n// path (the whole stream shifts by one cycle)\nreg              valid_d_q;\nreg [ 2:0]       idx_d_q;\nreg [ 7:0]       oh_d_q;\nreg [DATA_W-1:0] data0_d_q, data1_d_q, data2_d_q, data3_d_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_d_q <= 1'b0;\nelse\nbegin\n    valid_d_q <= data3_d_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// 32-entry RAM: sync write + one-hot decoded registered read\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose_ram #(\n     parameter DATA_W = 21\n)\n(\n    // Inputs\n     input           clk\n    ,input  [ [DATA_W-1:0] rd_q;\n\n// two-level one-hot decode of the read address (low fanout)\nwire [3:0] hh = 4'b0001 << {addr1_i[4], addr1_i[3]};\nwire [7:0] kk = 8'b00000001 << addr1_i[2:0];\n\nwire [31:0] en;\ngenvar gi, gj;\ngenerate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 4; gi = gi + 1)\n        for (gj = 0; gj < 8; gj = gj + 1)\n            assign en[gi*8+gj] = hh[gi] & kk[gj];\nendgenerate\n\nwire [DATA_W-1:0] 1)\n        assign or4[gq] = anded[gq*4+0] | anded[gq*4+1] | anded[gq*4+2] | anded[gq*4+3];\nendgenerate\n\nwire [DATA_W-1:0] rd_c;\ngenvar gg;\ngenerate\n    for (gg = 0; gg < DATA_W; gg = gg + 1)\n **Best result: 3.98M area_delay_product (design_v5.v), a 2.4× improvement over the original starting point (9.50M).**

### 1. Approaches tried
- **Baseline eval** of `starting_point.v` (verbatim core_jpeg) → 9.50M, delay 2222 ps, dominated by the transpose RAM read mux + ten 32×32 variable multipliers.
- **design_v1**: constant multipliers (operand register per C1..C7 instead of a muxed 32×32 variable multiplier), `/256` → add+shift, `ptr_conv` 64-case → bit concat, 21-bit transpose RAMs. Correct, but still 9.5M — the RAM read mux was the real critical path.
- **design_v3**: replaced the RAM read decode with a **two-level one-hot decode + AND-OR tree** (fanout ≤ 21 instead of ~130) inside the registered read. **2.13× win (4.46M)**.
- **design_v5**: v3 + restructured operand capture (even/odd coefficient banks, unconditional `i0` sum computation, ×C4 operand loaded from registered sums) + **registered one-hot pipeline tags** replacing all 3-bit case decodes. **Best: 3.98M, delay 988 ps.**
- **design_v8**: tried splitting the ×181/÷256 chain across beats 6/7 — passed but *slower* (4.65M) because it moved the adder into the multiply's operand path.

### 2. Highest-impact optimizations
1. **One-hot decoded RAM read** (v3): killed the ~1.4 µs high-fanout address-net + deep 32:1 mux path; delay 2222→1131 ps.
2. **Constant multipliers** replacing ten 32×32 variable multipliers with seven narrow constant ones per pass: area 4274→3948.
3. **Registered one-hot pipeline tags** (v5): eliminated 3→8 decode logic from every select path; delay 1131→988 ps.
4. Behavioral cleanups (free): `ptr_conv` = `{ptr[2:0],ptr[5:3]}`, signed `/256` = conditional add+shift, duplicate beat-3/4 loads dropped.

### 3. What didn't work
- **Datapath width narrowing** (v2): my bounds analysis was too aggressive — 32-bit LHS context silently wraps in Verilog; e.g. `t0+t7` needs 31 bits, not 30. Cost multiple debug cycles; reverted.
- **Early/pre-decoded read enables** (v4): the one-cycle-early decode chain had an off-by-one against the read schedule I couldn't nail within budget.
- **Splitting ×181/÷256 across beats** (v6/v7/v8): all either broke correctness (missed `o_t3` write; divide landing after an inter-block gap) or made the path longer (v8: multiply operand now includes the t6±t5 adder).
- `target_delay` sweeps: 1000 ps gave 4.26M vs 4.0M at default 500; 2200 ps didn't reduce area — Yosys just upsizes drivers. RTL structure, not the constraint, is the lever.

### 4. Lessons learned
- **Trace register-aliasing semantics cycle-by-cycle before rescheduling** — the original's same-cycle read-after-write aliases (e.g. `o_t0` at output beat 3) and gap-relative timing (last-row beat 7 → next beat 0 crosses the inter-block gap) are easy to break and hard to debug from TB errors alone.
- **Attack fanout and decode depth first** — the biggest win wasn't arithmetic but how the RAM address reached 256 storage bits.
- **Check LHS width contexts** when narrowing datapaths; truncation at assignment is not the same as 32-bit wrap.
- Next time: build a small self-checking C/Python model of the schedule before restructuring pipeline stages, and try one experiment per evaluation rather than bundling risky changes.
